# Ideas and outcomes

This is an Issue-centered index of ideas proposed in `Unjuno/agent-interface` and what happened to them. The [idea ledger](ideas/README.md) connects proposals to evidence, priorities, dependencies and experiments under the [repository consolidation plan](REPOSITORY_CONSOLIDATION.md). Documentation alone is not a reason to close an unresolved research Issue. Closed issues are included where useful. An Issue's closed state alone does not mean its hypothesis passed or failed.

This index complements, rather than duplicates, the [durable design-theses ledger](design-theses.md) and the detailed [research evidence ledger](../RESEARCH.md). The linked Issue, PR, and retained report remain authoritative for full scope and evidence.

## Status key

- **Proposed / open:** no result is implied.
- **Scoped result:** evidence supports only the stated fixture, method, and assumptions.
- **Recorded hold/failure labels:** preserve each allocation's recorded outcome; these labels describe that evidence only and do not assign work or suspend unrelated tasks.
- **Closed, unresolved:** workflow is closed, but the question was unanswered or continued elsewhere.

## Durable idea clusters

| Idea | Proposal | Current understanding |
|---|---|---|
| Agent-native interface north star | Issue [#4](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/4) asks whether the human-designed screenshot → reason → coordinate loop limits a capable model, and proposes an interface built around model-level intent while retaining universal GUI fallback. | #4 is an **OPEN design thesis**, not a single feature request or experiment. Its body points to #2 as an architecture-risk register and #3's selectable grid as one concrete proposal; the separate [visual-interface family record](ideas/visual-interface.md) and [execution agenda](ideas/visual-interface-agenda.md) keep #3/#5/#7/#8 as distinct, falsifiable interventions. The current Issue read has no comments or measured acceptance gate. Keep #4 open as the north star; assess candidate features on their own correctness, effect, and matched-cost evidence. Documentation or a component result does not establish the broad thesis. |
| Optional model-selectable visual grid | Issue [#3](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3) hypothesizes that a grid overlay can improve fine pointer localization while adding visual clutter and model-visible cost. | **OPEN / unverified** at the 2026-10-19 read. The proposal was archived by merged [PR #8924](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/8924), then reopened to keep the idea in active research organization; its latest comments retain the idea and correct its premature removal from the queue. The current [visual-interface agenda](ideas/visual-interface-agenda.md) reports no grid-specific implementation or result in its focused search and disambiguates unrelated JSON presentation/stream-receipt work. Next: audit any retained grid comparison and raw custody; if none exists, freeze a matched no-grid / always-on / selectable / coarse-to-fine comparison before execution. Keep this separate from #4's broad thesis and #5's observation-bound addressing/safety contract; do not close #3 on documentation alone. |
| Agent-native visual addresses and feedback contracts | Issue [#5](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5) is an open eight-part research menu spanning observation-bound addresses, presentation, anchors, servo, state/events, completion semantics, continuation and schemas. | Merged [PR #9016](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/9016) covers standard-caller construction under Fake X. Merged [PR #9099](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/9099) adds one scoped private-Xvfb pass through the public caller: synthetic window property `[1]`, key-up, and stale-alias refusal after destruction; its six earlier failed outcomes remain retained and the run chain is not seven independent samples. Its latest organizational-information-processing comparator is explicitly unverified. |

## Reactive macro and symbolic-interface family

Open [#6](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6) is a broad menu
for bounded local sensorimotor primitives and reactive continuation; open
[#56](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/56) isolates the
planner-authored symbolic GUI representation and cold/warm/repair economics.
They overlap in local continuation but are not interchangeable. The #56 A05
candidate failed its full correctness gate (A/B/D 12/12, C 9/12); the distinct
#7422 R02 contract reached 10/12 exact and 9/12 graph success and was rejected
on the frozen gates. A12–A14 are construction checks only; A14 accepted five
schemas, but one blank screenshot elicited task-2-like coordinates. None
establishes live GUI effect or full-task savings. The linked [family record](ideas/reactive-macro-symbolic-interface.md)
preserves each outcome and limit. Keep both Issues open. Merged [PR #7616](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/7616)
retains a separate #57 offline compiled-graph/pixel-handle composition package
whose frozen result remains `FAIL_SOURCE_BASE_LABEL_MISMATCH`. A read-only audit
reproduced that disposition without rerunning the consumed candidate. Four
relevant module blobs matched both cited source commits and package manifests
were verified after preserving bytes, but neither repairs the label mismatch or
missing candidate exit code/timestamps. This is not live GUI, task-effect,
efficiency evidence, or an acceptance result for #6/#56.

## Foundational open ideas

| Issue | Idea | Status / boundary |
|---|---|---|
| [#11](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/11) | Define a shared ABI and implementation-independent meanings for freshness, authority, completion, surface identity, and task success across execution routes. | Open, unverified. This is a cross-route contract question; it does not establish that current backends conform or authorize an ABI migration. Keep schema-repair diagnostics in #20 and visual-address details in #5 as separate scopes. |
| [#12](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/12) | Define a held-out cross-domain benchmark and independent oracle contract that resists known-task overfit and scorer leakage. | Open, unverified. Open successor [#8614](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8614) narrows one adaptive-tuning/finite-validation-cohort risk; that method question does not complete the parent benchmark/oracle contract. |
| [#18](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/18) | Preserve bounded competing target beliefs so uncertainty can inform action admission instead of collapsing to one possibly wrong match. | Open, unverified at the parent scope. Open successor [#7773](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/7773) asks whether candidate presentation order changes model-visible choice while list contents stay fixed; this does not establish calibrated beliefs or better target accuracy. |
| [#19](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/19) | Use non-authoritative shadow predictions to detect action/observation contradictions before live admission. | Open, unverified. A prediction must never count as an application effect or task completion; this remains a hypothesis about suitable action classes, not live safety evidence. |
| [#20](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/20) | Expose self-describing tool schemas and bounded, structured repair hints for invalid calls. | Open, unverified. Diagnostics should identify schema revision, invalid field, side-effect status and atomicity; ambiguous calls must not be silently rewritten. A learner-aware contrast-example refinement remains within this Issue and is not an API result. |
| [#21](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/21) | Expose observation-bound semantic actions for the currently active modal or modeful GUI state. | Open, unverified. Distinguish current-state affordances from #20's schema/repair diagnostics and #29's cross-modal disagreement handling. A locally completed program or prior screenshot cannot establish that an action remains valid in the current modal. |
| [#22](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/22) | Let the agent request bounded, useful feedback under a declared temporal condition instead of repeatedly polling generic screenshots or clocks. | Open, unverified at the user-visible behavior level. Open [PR #8237](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/8237) records a separate tail-finalization validation proposal; local completion, artifact delivery, and semantic completion remain distinct. No reduction in planner work or temporal-decision error is established here. |
| [#23](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/23) | Overlap independent intent tails when explicit dependency/resource footprints show they commute, while preserving one serialized input owner where required. | Open, unverified as a general capability. Merged [PR #1861](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/1861) retains one scoped private-X11 composition relevant to #23; it does not show arbitrary multi-agent parallelism, safe handling of unknown footprints, or general speedup. Keep separate from #16's commit/compensation semantics. |
| [#25](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/25) | Safely discover unknown GUI interaction semantics through bounded active probes that do not assume the probe itself is harmless. | Open, unverified for live-GUI transfer. Closed method issue [#1874](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/1874) records a scoped cost-optimal safe-probe tree result; method optimality does not establish safe application effects, useful discovery, or cross-domain adoption. Keep known affordance exposure (#21) separate from probing unknown behavior. |
| [#28](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/28) | Retrieve bounded, source-bound observation history and explicit gaps for temporal grounding without presenting replay as fresh state. | Open, unverified for agent-visible benefit. Its retained-artifact/liveness addendum is a proposal nested in #28, not an implemented eviction policy or result. Keep retrospective history distinct from #15 causal provenance, #22 future temporal contracts, and #7/#8 view selection/encoding. |
| [#29](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/29) | Define agreement, disagreement and staleness semantics across screenshot and UI-tree observations. | Open, unverified. Candidate contract fields include per-source identity/time, coordinate mapping and escalation; no multimodal action benefit or runtime policy is established. |
| [#30](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/30) | Recover observation capability after focus/authority mismatch without regaining input authority. | The latest Issue comment records `PASS_OBSERVATION_ONLY_RECOVERY_SEMANTICS_SCOPED` on 100,000 network-isolated synthetic cases: zero candidate/oracle mismatches and zero recovery-path input emissions. This is a semantic contract result only; no live focus detection, GUI task effect, latency, or production integration is established. |
| [#31](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/31) | Test relative viewpoint control for 3D environments such as camera navigation and interaction, rather than treating the scene as a flat screenshot. | Open, unverified. Successor [#8636](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8636) narrows a rotation-invariant visual-alignment subtask; its A01–A05 evidence and next discriminator are organized in the [family record](ideas/relative-view-rotation-features.md). It does not establish 3D navigation, camera/action integration, or Luanti task success. |
| [#32](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/32) | Resolve semantic targets and effect constraints into safe candidate actions, while retaining universal input fallback. | Open, unverified. The proposal requires AMBIGUOUS/NEEDS_DECISION when resolution is nonunique. Its later virtual-fixture transfer concerns path connectivity beyond endpoint proximity; no GUI result follows. |
| [#33](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/33) | Transfer a compact, source-bound handoff capsule across planner/session changes without reviving stale authority or replaying completed effects. | Open, unverified. Provenance/history are inputs, not a sufficient handoff contract; retain explicit unknown, in-flight, and non-replayable state. Literature addenda do not establish repository-specific summary accuracy. |
| [#34](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/34) | Make independently observed application effects explicit action outcomes instead of equating input acknowledgement or program completion with success. | Open, unverified as a general contract. Point-in-time postcondition, persistence, provenance, and independent task scoring are distinct; no one app-specific receipt establishes a universal effect outcome. |
| [#35](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/35) | Preserve bounded entity identity across frames while objects move, are occluded, or are replaced by visually identical incarnations. | Open, unverified at the general tracker scope. The Issue cites an incarnation-replacement boundary under [#347](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/347) / [PR #351](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/351); that separate fixed-scale resolver test does not establish a reusable cross-frame entity track. |
| [#37](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/37) | Let a planner request layout-independent semantic keyboard intents without guessing backend key names or modifier encodings. | Open, unverified. Shortcut resolution and literal/IME text insertion are different operations; an IME preedit or candidate-selection sequence cannot be reduced to a physical shortcut mapping without a separate text contract. |
| [#40](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/40) | Negotiate session-scoped capabilities and discover a safe fallback before attempting unsupported observation or action routes. | Open, unverified as to truthful completeness of the offered capability set. The cited #1919 snapshot result covers currentness, scope, authority=false and fallback semantics; it does not prove that a reduced/stripped offer faithfully represents producer capability. |
| [#41](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/41) | Let an agent attach bounded machine-checkable invariants to a long-running intent or action group. | Open, unverified. The proposal aims to detect drift before false completion; it does not establish continuous enforcement, runtime authority, or a production invariant mechanism. |
| [#44](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/44) | Resume from durable semantic checkpoints so interruption does not repeat completed GUI effects or assume transient progress persisted. | Open, unverified. This is distinct from #33's planner-context handoff and #16's transactional compensation; checkpoint placement must bind independently observed durable state, not planner memory alone. |
| [#45](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/45) | Reuse scoped semantic target handles with explicit revalidation as layout, camera, or object state changes. | Open, unverified at the general target-handle lifecycle. Successor [#8675](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8675) tests a structural-revision matching subproblem; it does not make a stale handle fresh or grant authority. Keep separate from #35's cross-frame identity tracking. |
| [#46](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/46) | Record a causal, auditable timing envelope across observation, planner, tool, input, effect, and recovery boundaries. | Open, unverified for real two-domain timing. Merged [PR #5755](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/5755) retained a synthetic endpoint-coverage audit; it does not establish provider latency, real task timing, or an end-to-end efficiency effect. |
| [#47](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/47) | Attribute external state changes to the agent, another process, a human, or an unknown actor without crediting or overwriting unowned effects. | Open, unverified as a general attribution guarantee. Later compromise analyses constrain how finely actor classes can be distinguished from available trusted witnesses; attribution must remain UNKNOWN when provenance is insufficient. |
| [#48](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/48) | Expose evidence-backed environment/target readiness states so loading, empty, black, or failed-startup views are not mistaken for target absence. | Open, unverified for live applications. Successor [#1358](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/1358) / merged [PR #1361](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/1361) records `PASS_READINESS_ACTION_ADMISSION_COMPOSITION_SCOPED` on a finite synthetic transition set; no live readiness detector or application task result follows. |
| [#49](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/49) | Expose scoped semantic caret and selection state before text-editing actions that could overwrite the wrong range. | Open, unverified as a general editor contract. Merged [PR #6943](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/6943) preserves one edit-offset boundary result and remote tree/readback; this does not establish complete caret/selection truth across apps, IMEs, or arbitrary text operations. |
| [#51](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/51) | Expose evidence-backed pre-action cost and assurance profiles so a planner can compare valid routes. | Open, unverified. This is a route-selection contract, not an estimate of current route latency or a permission to trade correctness for speed. A later refinement asks whether successful routes also expose perturbation tolerance; that remains unverified. |
| [#53](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/53) | Make adaptive acquisition accounting branch-complete inside the shared caller, including optional calls and recovery branches. | Open, unverified for full cost comparison. Existing first-anchor and wrong-anchor-recovery capability checks remain useful, but the top-level usage summary omits an optional third model call; the reported totals cannot support full-path comparison. Merged [PR #9251](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/9251) adds the offline ledger validator; this follow-up adds a durable generic provider/MCP attempt recorder with exception, timeout, raw-byte, unknown-usage and tool-call lineage checks. Both are local contracts only: no actual provider or orchestrator is instrumented, and their event stream is not reconciled against provider logs. PR #9251's merge unexpectedly auto-closed #53 through a GitHub closing-reference association; #53 was reopened and remains active. Next finite step: adapt the current provider/orchestrator and public MCP caller to the recorder, reconcile every attempt against provider logs, then resume the held matched comparison. Do not rerun prior GUI/model allocations just to fill the accounting gap. |
## Early research and discovery threads

| Issue | Idea / work | Current result and boundary |
|---|---|---|
| [#121](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/121) | Falsify generality of a MAP01 directional primitive before interface promotion. | V3 frozen successor has 0/18 formal cases and no measured policy action; six construction tests passed and a setup-only real-X11 transport smoke verified release. No efficacy result or promotion. |
| [#117](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/117) | Search simple MAP01 strategies under owner-deadline authority. | Retained real X11/v13 runs found plain-attack counts nonmonotonic across deadlines; a bounded back+left+attack candidate achieved 3/3 kills on two retained states with verified release. A nearby cutoff sweep remained nonmonotonic, so no minimum duration or general interface result is established. |
| [#105](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/105) | Use a one-variable ladder from input cutoff toward useful gameplay effect. | Successor #972 changed only immediate pre-input recapture: stable-target clicks remained correct 4/4 and target-swap inputs stopped 4/4 as `STALE_TARGET`. Scope is visibly changed targets; identical-pixel aliasing remains unresolved. |
| [#104](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/104) | Discover minimal mechanisms before composing a larger architecture. | Open method guidance: isolate one falsifiable mechanism, retain the first result, inspect failures, and compose only when evidence identifies a need. Its latest [correction](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/104#issuecomment-5711408171) points to closed [#944](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/944) / merged [PR #945](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/945), whose retained decision is `HOLD_GRID_TOO_COARSE` and supersedes an earlier PASS wording. The corrected result does not replace any frozen allocation or grant a shared resource. |
| [#93](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/93) | Allow work independent of an unresolved application effect to continue under explicit dependencies. | The related #165 successor shows that flat resource IDs miss alias remaps and query predicates; typed READ/RESOLVE/QUERY/WRITE evidence is more precise than global epochs in finite fixtures. No live application authority or effect-safety result. |
| [#88](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/88) | Measure interruption telemetry and release timing on a retained MAP01 container lane. | In a narrow mechanism comparison, owner-deadline median verified-empty time was 48.986 ms versus 50.933 ms for two-phase; first full artifact and terminal were also earlier, but the study is a small retained fixture and not a formal recovery-efficacy or model result. |
| [#76](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/76) | Close the live MAP01 gate with duplicate-run and terminal-score checks. | Both retained live-02 archives pass five-field terminal value agreement, but the final sample payload timestamps precede their recorded start times by 7,624 ns and 8,446 ns. Value equality does not validate sample-acquisition timing; preserve that unresolved audit finding and do not infer recovery efficacy. |
| [#5276](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5276) | Test whether selective verification can retain critical-check coverage while reducing verifier count, latency, and cost. | Open benchmark proposal. Compare no verification, full checklist, deterministic selection, a small router, and rich planning on one frozen Agent Action corpus with an independent effect oracle. A learned router must beat the deterministic arm; no runtime-default or cross-domain claim is established. |
| [#3447](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3447) | Evaluate adaptive Local System-1 as a composed system while preserving rich-model intent and deterministic authority. | Keep the original standalone learned-backend rejection in [#3440](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3440) (merged [PR #4806](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/4806)): MLP missed its preregistered +10 percentage-point shifted-coverage margin and 0.99 actionable-precision threshold, while learned controls failed. The open follow-up asks a separate composed comparison against rich-model-only and deterministic local proposals; it has no result yet. |
| [#3440](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3440), [#3443](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3443), [#3444](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3444) | Explore bounded local backend candidates, learned residual controllers, and adaptive visual grounding. | Preserve #3440's standalone learned-backend rejection and the distinct open composed evaluation in #3447. The macro-plus-residual and visual-grounding proposals remain unverified; they do not grant execution authority or replace current evidence checks. |
| [#4158](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/4158) | Keep intent delegation to Local System-1 bounded, revisable, and subordinate to current evidence and deterministic authority. | Provisional architecture guidance, not an implementation or performance result. It preserves both explicit and learned delegation as candidates and points to separate issues for provenance, adaptation, and composed evaluation. |
| [#2531](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/2531) | Separate training-seed uncertainty from split/family uncertainty in visual adaptation evaluation. | Open unrun crossed-split successor to #2459: its five seeds reused one same-family split, so the zero false-positive count is not a population safety bound and split variation remains unmeasured. |
| [#2527](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/2527) | Observe a complete effect window before classifying a one-tic ATTACK action. | Open successor preserves #2477's one-state/one-sample `HOLD`; no observation at a single post-action point establishes that ATTACK has no effect. |
| [#2526](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/2526) | Isolate translation and Gaussian-noise augmentation after the confounded parent result. | Construction stopped before training because the source-bound X11 train/shift manifests and raw frames were unavailable. No model was trained and no GPU scientific result exists; preserve the parent 50.0% result without generalizing it to all augmentation. [Retained stop](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/2526#issuecomment-5751142351). |

|---|---|---|
| [#8905](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8905) | Distinguish evidence fixity, interpretability, auditability, re-execution, and independent reproduction after loss of an experiment's original runtime. | Open, unverified proposal, not a repository-wide preservation-failure claim. One host-only raw-auditor invocation per package returned #5749 PASS_METHOD_SCOPED (78 rows; 7/7 mutations rejected), #6530 PASS (8 rows), and #6590 HOLD_AUDIT_INTEGRITY (hash/refit/prediction mismatches under Python 3.14.5 / NumPy 2.5.2 versus pinned NumPy 1.26.4). No candidate ran; inputs were not copied, and network isolation, preservation-level classification, and fallback testing were not performed. The three-package offline method remains unverified. [Idea](ideas/claim-preservation.md).
| [#8904](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8904) / predecessor [#5513](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5513) | Mine candidate metamorphic relations from finite traces, then independently validate each relation within a supported applicability envelope. | Open, unverified successor to #5513's manually declared relations. Require held-out traces, semantic/authority mutants, and an independent oracle; a finite relation stays LIKELY_SCOPED, not universal. Mining frequency is not proof, cannot label effects, and grants no authority. No allocation or result. [Note](ideas/trace-mined-metamorphic-relations.md).
| [#8903](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8903) / predecessors [#5590](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5590) and [#8846](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8846) | Test whether a route-blind weak outcome proxy can tighten missing-route-outcome bounds without using predictions as labels. | Open, unverified finite-method successor. Compare assumption-free and ascertainment-sensitivity bounds with proxy-constrained intervals; tighten only when frozen assumptions pass independent checks. A synthetic result cannot validate real proxy assumptions, and proxy outputs never become outcome labels. No allocation or result is recorded. [Note](ideas/weak-shadow-route-outcome-bounds.md).
| [#8902](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8902) / predecessor [#5593](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5593) | Represent recoverable pauses and resumed work as within-task transitions when measuring route outcomes over time. | Open, unverified measurement successor to #5593's first-terminal curves. Keep #8068's history-conditioned repair estimand separate and reuse only compatible event-ledger conventions. No real cohort, recovery-safety, or policy-benefit result. [Note](ideas/recoverable-pause-transition-accounting.md).

| [#8900](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8900) / predecessors [#7802](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/7802), [#7807](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/7807), and [#5970](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5970) | Test whether an independently rooted, read-only evidence path can resolve some post-crash UNKNOWN_RECONCILE cases while action and retry authority stay revoked. | Open, unverified finite-method proposal with no implementation/result. ## Closed ideas and successor lineage

Closure does not erase evidence. Preserve [#1968](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/1968)'s `HOLD_NO_FORMAL_CONTAINER`; its separate [later scoped report](../research/analysis/serialized_attention_duplicate_label_successor_1968_v1/REPORT.md) found exact reconstruction and smaller serialized packages on a tiny fixture, but used truth-derived crops and makes no automatic-attention, model, or GUI claim. The later #6353 duplicate disposition and its canonical open lineage are detailed below.

- [#8891](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/8891) was closed into still-open [#6650](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6650) to avoid a duplicate lane. Retain its recent-minimum sojourn estimator as a candidate signal within #6650; do not count it as an independent experiment. The [persistent-sojourn note](ideas/persistent-sojourn-gating.md) preserves the existing #6650 `FAIL_HYPOTHESIS` result and its audit limit.

- [#6427](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6427) was closed as duplicate routing after #6410's comment applied `ISSUE_FAILURE_CLASSIFICATION.md`: an auditor/harness failure stays under the same question unless a distinct scientific hypothesis warrants a successor. No separate allocation or audit ran. #6410's `STOP_METHOD_FAILURE` and raw remain unchanged; any corrected audit is additive under #6410 and requires a new exact-main freeze/resource gate.
- [#6319](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6319) closed after its proposed allocation ID/seed collided with active #6322: candidate/CUDA/auditor/retries all 0, no CUDA call. This is an allocation STOP, not a scientific test of six-worker sharing. Open successor [#6329](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6329) remains `PREPARED_DEFERRED_UNASSIGNED`: open Draft [PR #6428](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/6428) refroze the WSLc allocation-10 preparation to current main; the earlier standalone CPU-only preflight passed 10/10, but formal in-window construction, candidate, CUDA workers, auditor, and retries remain 0/0/0/0/0. GPU interval is unassigned; no memory-safety or throughput result.
- [#6324](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/6324) closed as duplicate/not planned after confirming #4850 had already run the exact seeds, arms and CUDA protocol. #6324 itself used no candidate/CUDA/auditor (0/0/0); its WSLc CPU construction 5/5 was environment prep only. Preserve #4850's scientific `STOP_NO_CONSTRUCTION_COMPETENCE` (train/base 0.50/0.50; held-positive acceptance 0.00) and separate posthoc auditor-v2 improvement without upgrading the original audit record. Open #6296 continues only with a distinct finite-difference construction question and its own runtime/slot gates; no result from #6324.

A multi-mount preflight failed and a no-mount smoke passed; a fresh mount-only preflight is the next gate. No formal audit outcome is recorded here. A pass would audit receipts, not actual cgroup enforcement or memory relief.

## Evidence recovery and publication lineage

Readback: 2026-10-02 UTC. These links separate retained results, source custody, and missing publication; the owning Issue’s later corrections remain authoritative.

- **Canonical #5274 versus vj01/#5282:** [The general reducer](../research/verification/evidence_reducer_5274_v1/README.md) retains `PASS_TYPED_REDUCER_FINITE_SCOPED` and its formal archive through [PR #5283](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/pull/5283). The separate [vj01 source archive](../research/archive/verification_evidence_vj01_5274_5282_20261002/README.md) preserves the duplicate general preparation’s `STOP_DUPLICATE_SCOPE_BEFORE_FORMAL` and #5282 source only. [#5282’s latest custody update](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5282#issuecomment-5955862972) records main archive plus exact historical tag replacing the delivery ref; its distinct 47,644-byte raw packet is still missing and publication remains `HOLD_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE`. Do not attribute that gap to the canonical general reducer or treat source preservation as a recovered result.
- **Closed #1927 → open #3257:** [The publication audit](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/3257#issuecomment-5860470045), reaffirmed in [#672](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/672#issuecomment-5955921871), leaves the reported 16-pair/80-row successor PASS comment-only: exact result, audits and successor manifest are absent from main. The publication HOLD is owned by #3257; #1927’s original STOP remains unchanged. Recovery needs the existing exact bytes, not another run or reconstructed rows.
- **#4327:** [The latest recovery check](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/4327#issuecomment-5944454624) retains `HOLD_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE`: the branch contains an isolated auditor, not the complete study/raw/restorer package. The Issue’s preservation and publication boundary remains controlling; private/offline copies are unknown.

## Sources of truth

- Governing direction: open [#5280](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/5280) says to solve computer control with the smallest sufficient mechanism and an existing/simple comparator; open [#2789](https://github.com/Unjuno/agent-interface/issues/2789) says not to turn a worker's local publication failure into a new research objective or fleet-wide blocker. See [principles](principles.md), [CURRENT_GOAL](CURRENT_GOAL.md), and [INTEGRATION_PLAN](INTEGRATION_PLAN.md).
- [Design Theses and Idea Ledger](design-theses.md) — durable idea/thesis summaries.
- [RESEARCH.md](../RESEARCH.md) — evidence ledger and detailed results.
- [CURRENT_GOAL.md](CURRENT_GOAL.md) and [ROADMAP.md](../ROADMAP.md) — current direction and roadmap, not result ledgers.

### Closed parents continued by open successors or reopened audit records

A parent's closure does not close a distinct transfer question. These checked lineages keep completed finite results, reopened provenance audits, and broader successor scope separate.

A separate local execution disclosed candidate once and auditor twice after another worker had consumed the globally one-shot allocation, against a different source/main identity and Python version. Closure was premature because that duplicate-run provenance had not been reconciled. Keep #6184 open for an additive audit/disposition; do not count the duplicate as a second scientific result or rerun the allocation. Broader real-GUI transfer remains open in #6181. No GUI/GPU/model result. |

These entries summarize documented outcomes, not closure labels. Successor STOPs remain STOPs and do not erase predecessor results.

This curated page is not exhaustive of all repository Issues. Keep its thematic summaries current, and use the full inventory to discover open/closed research threads; preserve full raw logs and detailed claims in linked Issues, PRs, and reports.
